- _symbolic _____z_Xx 12CoreGraphics7CGFloatV
CStrings:
+ " + X more string, where X is the number of hidden entries"
+ "%@ = shortcut name"
+ "%s finished loading with %ld sections, %ld entries."
+ "A label to indicate the action is only Apple-internal, not public."
+ "Accessibility hint"
+ "Accessibility label"
+ "Accessibility label for the color picker section"
+ "Accessibility label for the folder name field"
+ "Accessibility label for the folder symbol picker"
+ "Accessibility label for the home screen preview section"
+ "Accessibility label for the icon type section"
+ "Accessibility label for the image picker section"
+ "Accessibility label for the symbol picker section"
+ "Accessibility title used for each row in the Privacy tab to describe the picker"
+ "Add to Home Screen image icon type."
+ "Add to Home Screen symbol icon type."
+ "Alert button"
+ "Alert message when deleting a folder containing a single shortcut"
+ "Alert message when deleting a folder containing multiple shortcuts"
+ "Alert message when deleting a shortcut from the user's device."
+ "Alert message when deleting a shortcut from the user's iCloud devices."
+ "Alert message when deleting multiple shortcuts from the user's device."
+ "Alert message when deleting multiple shortcuts from the user's iCloud devices."
+ "Alert title"
+ "Alert title asking the user to confirm deleting a shortcut while deleting a folder"
+ "Alert title asking the user to confirm deleting multiple shortcuts while deleting a folder"
+ "Alert title for creating iCloud link"
+ "Alert title for deleting stored content"
+ "Auto Shortcuts Siri setting title"
+ "Auto Shortcuts preferences title"
+ "Auto Shortcuts section header"
+ "Auto Shortcuts settings done button title"
+ "Automation action"
+ "Automation enablement toggle label"
+ "Automation filter option"
+ "Automation filter option for active automations"
+ "Automation filter option for inactive automations"
+ "Button"
+ "Button at footer of privacy settings to reset privacy settings for a shortcut"
+ "Button in Add to Home Screen view"
+ "Button in Share Sheet; add this shortcut to home screen"
+ "Button in Workflow settings; add this shortcut to home screen"
+ "Button in Workflow settings; customize this shortcut by showing import questions"
+ "Button or title"
+ "Button that deletes an automation without deleting the shortcut it runs"
+ "Button that shows all collection search results"
+ "Button title"
+ "Button title to switch from the generative conversation to the action editor"
+ "Button title to trust a shortcut that was previously disabled due to potential malware content"
+ "Button title; don’t add shortcut to library"
+ "Button to keep both shortcuts when you try to import a shortcut with the same name"
+ "Button to leave the full-screen editor and return to the split view"
+ "Button to replace the older shortcut with the newer shortcut when you try to import a shortcut with the same name"
+ "Button; %@ is replaced with a collection name"
+ "Cancel button"
+ "Cancel button used in Settings error alert."
+ "Chosen action in smart shortcut picker section row when further configuration is not available at this point."
+ "Chosen action in smart shortcut picker section row, parameter is action name."
+ "Confirmation message for deleting stored value"
+ "Confirmation message when deleting a voice shortcut"
+ "Confirmation message when trusting a quarantined shortcut"
+ "Conflicts scroll view"
+ "Customize shortcut"
+ "Date bucket - this month"
+ "Date bucket - this week"
+ "Date bucket - today"
+ "Date bucket - yesterday"
+ "Default name for a newly created stored value"
+ "Default title for alert shown when automation creation fails"
+ "Default title for alert shown when automation modification fails"
+ "Delete Automation"
+ "Delete all shortcuts"
+ "Delete button"
+ "Delete this automation?"
+ "Description for privacy settings, listing apps and websites the shortcut can access"
+ "Description for storage section in shortcut details"
+ "Description of selected app icon in icon picker; %1$@ = app icon name"
+ "Description of selected glyph icon and color in icon picker; %1$@ = glyph icon name; %2$@ = color name"
+ "Disabled app subtitle in Smart Shortcut Picker"
+ "Discoverability or accessibility title"
